# The AAS join, 2015–2024: the phase-1 and phase-2a report

Generated by `npx tsx tools/acta-report.ts` from `data/documents/*.json` and `tools/fixtures/acta/` on 2026-09-12 — the
deliverable of phase 1 of [#25](https://github.com/CatholicOS/cmddr/issues/25) as the
[phase-1 spec](../specs/2026-09-12-acta-reference-design.md) §4.4 defines it, extended by phase 2a as the
[AAS-only documents spec](../specs/2026-09-13-acta-only-documents-design.md) §6 defines it. Every entry of the *Acta Summi Pontificis* part of
the *Index documentorum chronologico ordine digestus* of the ten annual *Index generalis* PDFs (`tools/fixtures/acta/README.md`
records extractor and retrieval) is parsed (`tools/src/acta/index.ts`), classified (`categories.ts`) and matched to a harvested
document by issuer, date and incipit (`match.ts`, with the curated index corrections of `curation.ts`). What matched is written
as `document.acta`; what did not, in a category the registry creates from the *Acta*, becomes a document of its own unless a
rule or the duplicate guard holds it (`create.ts`, §8 and §9); everything else is listed here. The report re-runs the join and
the creator over the shelf records alone and checks the creator's output against the AAS-only records the data carries (§8).
The *belief* printed beside each unmatched entry is the report script's heuristic; the reading in the prose is the author's.

*Claimed twice* counts documents two entries of the year both match (neither is written; §5; one pair spans 2021 and 2022,
so the years sum to one more than the total). *Created* counts the AAS-only documents made from the year's entries (§8) and
*Held* the entries in a harvested category that were neither matched nor created, with the reason (§9); the two sum, with
*Matched*, to *In harvested categories* (a document claimed twice holds both its entries). *Francis documents without an
entry* counts the harvested shelf documents dated in the volume year that carry no `acta` (§11); the December ones belong to the next
volume, and 2024's December acts to the 2025 index, which does not exist yet.

### The reading

1. **Where the join fires it is evidenced.** 225 matches: 210 the only candidate of the class on the
   date, 14 told apart by the incipit slug, 0 by the toponym (no two constitutions of one date needed it), and
   1 by a curated override (`curation.ts`: *Finis et modus*, AAS 116 (2024) 189, which the class rule had sent to the
   decree of the same date because the letter it names is on apost_letters alone — a measured harm of the discussion #30
   question). Every one rests on an index line quoted in §12,
   and every Francis encyclical and exhortation of the ten volumes is among them. Nothing was written that the rules could not
   evidence: the 40 ambiguous entries (§4) and the 12 documents two entries claim (§5) stay without a reference.
2. **The Francis shelves are selections, and the index is the record.** The index names 117 apostolic constitutions and 206
   apostolic letters over 2015–2024; vatican.va's apost_constitutions and apost_letters shelves carry 49 and 59 (plain) for the
   same pontificate, and the *bulls* shelf none of the 63 canonisation decretals. Almost every unmatched entry of §6 is a
   **shelf gap** of this kind, and every one prints an incipit, a date and a page: phase 2a made 266 of them documents (§8)
   and held 376 with a reason (§9).
3. **The index's chronology is not reliable at the ditto mark — and once it was the parser.** Two acts the shelf holds are
   entered under the wrong month (*Vultum Dei quaerere* reads 29 July for 29 June; *De concordia inter Codices* 31 March for
   31 May) and are matched by the curated corrections of `curation.ts`, each quoting the act's own dating formula. The third
   phase-1 reading, *Episcopalis communio* at 15 May, was the parser's: the index prints `Sept. » Chengden.:` with no day two
   entries before it, the line was skipped, and the ditto months after it inherited May. Read as printed, *Episcopalis communio*
   is 15 September 2018 and *Prisrensis-Priscensis* 5 September 2018 (§3 still lists the day-less line as a defect).
4. **The shelf truncates incipits; the index does not.** *Ad aptius consulendum* (2015-03-19) is *Ad aptius* on the shelf, and
   *Ad aptius* (2015-12-18) is *Ad aptius consulendum* — the truncation runs both ways — and *Nos, qui successimus* is *Nos, qui*.
   All three are ambiguous only for this reason (§4). The 25 Tarragona beatification letters of 13 October 2013, provisional on
   the shelf because vatican.va prints no incipit for them, each have one in the index (*Spiritus Domini*, *Qui enim voluerit*,
   …); telling the seven shelf records apart needs the documents' own text, which is a RECOVERED_INCIPITS job for the owner.
5. **Messages need an occasion, not a date.** The index carries 391 *Nuntii* (video messages included) and 113
   matched, among them the twenty Christmas and Easter *Urbi et Orbi* the index files as *Nuntius et Benedictio* (the one
   category that maps to two registry classes, `message` and `urbi-et-orbi`). Where two *Nuntii* fall on the date of one series
   message the match is withheld (§5), and two series messages on one date are ambiguous (§4, 8 December 2016): the entry's
   description names the *Dies Mundialis* every time, so a series discriminator is the phase-2 rule to add — or the
   pont-messages harvest (#4) first, which gives every occasional *Nuntius* a candidate.
6. **Filing differences, not defects.** Three acts the index files as *Motu proprio datae* are on apost_letters only, so the
   registry carries no `motu-proprio` characteristic and the class rule declines them (§6, discussion #30); the guard holds all
   three (§9: one as a class mismatch by its title, two as possible identities of the incipit-less shelf records). The four
   *sub plumbo* letters of 28 November 2020 (cardinalatial titles) are on no harvested shelf and are created as papal bulls,
   provisional, from the index (§8). Whether the characteristic should follow the index is the owner's call; the matcher was
   not loosened to decide it.
7. **Source defects the parser sees** (§3): a constitution whose day is not printed (*Chengden.*, 2018) and one whose page is
   not (*Ioinvillen.*, 2024) — both in a harvested category, both unrecoverable from the text layer — and OCR slips elsewhere
   (a page split `76 4`, ditto marks read `? ?`, a page glued to a footnote digit). None is absorbed.
8. **The shelves' gaps are now documents, and the guard held 45.** 266 AAS-only documents (§8): 151 apostolic-letter, 76 papal-bull+apostolic-constitution, 39 papal-bull; 75 provisional (a constitution the index names
   by toponym only), and the rest minted from the index's incipit exactly as a shelf incipit mints. None carries `incipitLang`:
   the index wraps the beatification letters' Latin incipits in guillemets as it does vernacular ones (190 of the 191 minted
   records are quoted), so the guillemets mark a quotation, not a language, and the shelf harvest sets the field nowhere (spec §4,
   corrected in PR #32).
   The guard is wider than the matcher on purpose: vatican.va files canonisation decretals on apost_letters as *Lettera
   Decretale* with no incipit, so *possible identity* looks at every incipit-less record of the date whatever its genre, and
   26 decretals of 6 canonisation days are held rather than doubled (§9). A hold is released by hand; a duplicate is not.

## 5. Documents claimed by two entries

One document is the only candidate of its class on the date for two entries of the index. One page opens one act, so both
claims are withheld and reported (`match.ts`): every case is two *Nuntii* on the date of one series message, except the
two beatification letters of 15 August 2016 that share a provisional record.

## 6. Unmatched entries in harvested categories

Each is a candidate gap in the shelf harvest, a parser defect or a matcher defect; the belief column says which. The two
dominant shapes are both **shelf gaps**: vatican.va's Francis shelves for constitutions and apostolic letters are
selections, not the record — the index names 117 apostolic constitutions and 206 apostolic letters over the ten years
against 49 and 59 on the shelves.

## 8. Created from the Acta

The AAS-only documents (`tools/src/acta/create.ts`): an entry the join left unmatched, in a category the registry creates from
the *Acta* (encyclicals, exhortations, constitutions, motu proprio, apostolic letters, and the bull class — decretals, *sub plumbo*
letters — insofar as the pope's shelf for the class is harvested), dated within the pontificate, not ambiguous, not claiming a
document another entry claims, named by no curated hold, and clear of the duplicate guard (§9). Each becomes a record with the
id the shelf's rule would mint from the index's incipit (provisional, `{genre}-{date}`, for a constitution the index names by
toponym only), the index entry as its title, `source.shelf` `aas/{year}` with `url: null`, and `acta` as its citation and its source.

The data carries **266** AAS-only records and the creator, re-run here over the shelf records, produces **266** — the same set, entry for entry, and the same records field for field (ids aside, which the collision and ordinal passes assign).

### Per year and category

| Category | 2015 | 2016 | 2017 | 2018 | 2019 | 2020 | 2021 | 2022 | 2023 | 2024 | Total |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Constitutiones Apostolicae | 0 | 1 | 5 | 10 | 23 | 7 | 3 | 3 | 14 | 10 | 76 |
| Litterae Apostolicae | 7 | 10 | 15 | 16 | 37 | 13 | 15 | 14 | 18 | 6 | 151 |
| Litterae Apostolicae sub plumbo datae | 0 | 0 | 0 | 0 | 0 | 4 | 0 | 0 | 0 | 0 | 4 |
| Litterae Decretales | 0 | 4 | 10 | 5 | 13 | 0 | 1 | 1 | 0 | 1 | 35 |
| **Total** | **7** | **15** | **30** | **31** | **73** | **24** | **19** | **18** | **32** | **17** | **266** |

By issuer: `rp:benedict-xvi` 6; `rp:francis-i` 260. By class: apostolic-letter 151; papal-bull+apostolic-constitution 76; papal-bull 39. Provisional (toponym only): 75.

**Circumscription material.** Of the 76 AAS-born constitutions, the index describes 56 with *conditur*, *erigitur*,
*dismembrato* or *extollitur* (the spec's four words), and 75 with any of the erection, elevation or union verbs below; none carries a
`keywords` or `actKind` yet (spec §4) — the count is the material for a circumscription-keyword PR of its own.

| Verb | Constitutions |
|---|---|
| conditur / constituitur / erigitur (erection) | 50 |
| dismembrato / dismembratis (from another see's territory) | 24 |
| extollitur / evehitur / attollitur (elevation) | 26 |
| iunguntur / in unam (union) | 7 |
| none of these | 1 |

Without any of the verbs: `mag:francis-i/papal-bull-2021-02-11` (Sucren.: Sucrensis Metropolitana Sedes in Bolivia Primatis titulo honestatur…).

### Shelf ids re-minted by densification

**None.** No AAS-only provisional record shares an ordinal group (issuer, genre, date) with a shelf provisional record, and
the shelf's ordinals are what a pass over the shelf records alone assigns; no minted shelf id gained a full-date suffix either
(the guard's *same incipit elsewhere* holds an entry whose incipit a shelf record of the year already carries).

collision and ordinal passes of the harvest.

## 9. Held

Every entry of a harvested category that was neither matched nor created, per reason. The four guard reasons of spec §3 —
*class mismatch*, *possible identity*, *near-miss*, *same incipit elsewhere* — carry the candidate ids; the rest are the
creation conditions of §2 (a category not created from the *Acta*, a shelf not harvested for the pope, an act of an earlier
pontificate, an ambiguous entry, a document two entries claim) and the one case the identifier scheme cannot resolve (two
entries of one date with one incipit). A hold is released by a curated row, never by loosening a rule.

| Reason | Held | Of which per category |
|---|---|---|
| Category not created from the Acta (the shelf that carries the class is not harvested) | 266 | Epistulae Apostolicae 12; Nuntii 173; Nuntii televisifici 81 |
| Date before the pontificate | 1 | Litterae Apostolicae 1 |
| Ambiguous (§4) | 40 | Constitutiones Apostolicae 3; Litterae Apostolicae 30; Litterae Apostolicae Motu proprio datae 5; Nuntii 2 |
| Claimed twice (§5) | 24 | Litterae Apostolicae Motu proprio datae 2; Nuntii 20; Nuntii televisifici 2 |
| Guard: class mismatch | 1 | Litterae Apostolicae Motu proprio datae 1 |
| Guard: possible identity | 41 | Litterae Decretales 26; Litterae Apostolicae 6; Constitutiones Apostolicae 7; Litterae Apostolicae Motu proprio datae 2 |
| Guard: same incipit elsewhere | 1 | Constitutiones Apostolicae 1 |
| Id collision | 2 | Litterae Decretales 2 |
| **Total** | **376** | |

## 11. Francis documents with no AAS entry

Harvested Francis shelf documents dated within the ten volume years that carry no `acta`, per genre. A document dated in December
belongs to the next year's volume (December 2024 to the 2025 index, which does not exist yet); a decree vatican.va files on
motu_proprio is a *Decretum* in the index (not harvested); and an act the index lists on the date under another class is an
entry of §4–§7. The formal genres are listed, since an encyclical or constitution absent from AAS would be surprising.

| Genre class | Without an entry | Of which dated December | Harvested in the window |

| apostolic-letter+motu-proprio | 25 | 0 | 72 |
| message | 21 | 4 | 111 |
| apostolic-letter | 12 | 2 | 27 |
| homily | 9 | 0 | 9 |
| papal-bull+apostolic-constitution | 4 | 1 | 31 |
| urbi-et-orbi | 2 | 1 | 21 |
